CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告
During FY2018/2019, the Vitasoy Group continued
to deliver strong growth in both revenue and profit
as a result of significant growth in our biggest
market of Mainland China. We will continue to
drive long term sustainable growth.

得益於最大的中國內地市場的大幅度業務增長，
維他奶集團於二零一八╱二零一九財政年度的收入
及溢利繼續錄得強勁增長。我們將繼續推動長遠
持久增長。

                                                                             Annual Report 2018/2019 年度報告   9
Group Chief Executive
Officer’s Report/
Business Review
集團行政總裁報告╱業務回顧

In FY2018/2019, the Vitasoy Group saw another
year of double-digit growth in both revenue and
profit. Revenue growth was broad-based across
markets with major contribution coming from
Mainland China. We will significantly increase
investments to secure foundation for sustainable
growth in long term.

維他奶集團於二零一八╱二零一九財政年度的收入
及溢利均再次錄得雙位數增長。各市場均有廣泛收
入增長，尤以中國內地市場貢獻最大。我們將會大
幅增加投資，為長遠可持續增長的目標打好基礎。
Group Chief Executive Officer’s Report/ Business Review
集團行政總裁報告╱業務回顧




FY2018/2019 has been another year of double-digit growth in             於二零一八╱二零一九財政年度，收入及溢利均
both revenue and profit. The Group grew revenue by 16%, behind          再次錄得雙位數增長。各市場均有廣泛增長，尤
broad-based growth across markets with the most significant             以中國內地市場貢獻最大，帶動本集團收入增長
contribution coming from Mainland China. Profit from operations         16%。在富盈利的產品及地區銷售組合以及高產
grew even faster by 19%, with a favourable mix across the portfolio     能利用率的各種利好因素配合下，經營溢利更加
and markets and high capacity utilisation. The revenue and profit       速增長 19%。倘假設下半年人民幣貶值並無對中
from operation would actually grow 18% and 21% respectively in          國內地業務因就呈報目的換算為港幣而令表現造
constant currency terms if the depreciation of Renminbi (RMB)           成影響，按固定匯率基準計算，收入及經營溢利
in second half of the year had not affected the performance of          實際上分別增長 18% 及 21%。
Mainland China when translated into Hong Kong dollars (HKD)
for reporting.

On sustainability, this year we have already achieved most of           可持續發展方面，我們於本年度已達成大部分二
the Key Performance Indicators (“KPIs”) that were originally set        零二零╱二零二一財政年度原定的關鍵表現指
for FY2020/2021. Therefore, we have reset and broadened our             標。因此，如可持續發展報告所述，我們已重新

Business Outlook                                                     業務前景
We anticipate continued solid growth in FY2019/2020, albeit at a     我們預期業務於二零一九╱二零二零財政年度繼
more modest pace. At the same time, the Group will significantly     續穩健增長，但步伐將較為緩和。同時，本集團
increase investments in brand equity, infrastructure and             將根據長遠可持續增長的計劃，大幅增加於品牌
organisational capacity and capabilities according to our plan to    價值、基礎設施及組織管理能力及實力等方面的
secure long term sustainable growth.                                 投資。

VITA brands. We will make further investments in brand equity        增加投資建立品牌價值，加強基礎設施以及創新
building, infrastructure readiness and innovation for the future.    產品。由於內地市場產品組合的人均消費相對較

market continues to hold significant potential for growth.

     Conclusion                                                              總結
     We will continue to execute our strategy with increased attention       我們將繼續貫徹執行業務策略，並加倍專注於須
     to its long term requirements of investments in brand equity,           在建立品牌價值、基礎設施及組織管理實力等方
     infrastructure and organisational capabilities to secure long term      面作出長期投資的需求，以爭取達致長遠增長。
     success. The market, retail and shopper context is favourable. Our      市場環境、零售氣氛及顧客喜好等利好因素、企
     corporate and brand positioning are relevant. Our products are          業及品牌定位正面良好、我們的產品以植物成分
     delivering plant-based nutrition and taste. Our operating model         為主且營養豐富又美味，加上經營模式逐漸轉向
     is increasingly integrating sustainability at its core. This makes us   以可持續發展為重心，這些都令我們對未來發展
     confident about the future.                                             充滿信心。


     I would like to most sincerely thank our Executive Chairman and         本人謹此衷心感謝執行主席及董事會所給予的寶
     our Board of Directors for sharing their invaluable experience,         貴經驗、悉心指導及真知灼見，以及業務夥伴對
     guidance and insight; as well as our business partners for their        我們一如既往的信心及持續的進步。最後，本人
     continued confidence and improvement. Finally, I must thank our         謹此感謝全體員工的盡忠職守及貢獻。
     staff for their dedication and contribution.

企業管治報告




Vitasoy International Holdings Limited (the “Company”) is firmly                維他奶國際集團有限公司（「本公司」）堅守高水
committed to a high level of corporate governance and adherence                 平的企業管治，並時刻遵守注重具透明度、獨立
to the governance principles and practices emphasising                          性、問責、負責與公平之管治原則及常規。本公
transparency, independence, accountability, responsibility                      司在適當時候檢討及修訂該等原則及常規，以反
and fairness. These principles and practices are reviewed and                   映不斷轉變的監管規定及企業管治發展。董事會
                                                                                相信，高標準的企業管治對維他奶集團持續其長
revised as appropriate to reflect the ever changing regulatory
                                                                                遠表現，為各股東、公眾投資者及其他持份者創
requirements and corporate governance development. The
                                                                                造價值等，乃不可或缺的關鍵元素。
Board of Directors (the “Board”) believes that the high standards
of corporate governance is the essential core for sustaining
Vitasoy Group’s long term performance and value creation for our
shareholders, the investing public and the other stakeholders.


Corporate Governance Practices                                                  企業管治常規
The Company has, throughout the year ended 31st March 2019,                      本公司於截至二零一九年三月三十一日止年度
complied with the code provisions set out in the Corporate                       一直遵守香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規
Governance Code (the “CG Code”) contained in Appendix 14                         則（「上市規則」）附錄十四所載之企業管治守則
of the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock                   （「企業管治守則」）中之守則條文。
Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Listing Rules”).

     Board of Directors                                                                    董事會
     The general management of the Company’s business is vested in                         董事會負責本公司業務之整體管理工作，並已成
     the Board. The Board has established various committees to carry                      立不同之委員會來履行由董事會委派且需具備特
     out specified functions assigned by the Board of Directors which                      定專業知識範疇之特定職能。董事會委任本公司
     require specialized area of expertise. The Board has delegated the                    之執行董事及高層管理人員負責管理本公司日常
                                                                                           事務。然而，根據《公司條例》、上市規則、企業
     day-to-day management power of the Company to the Executive
                                                                                           管治守則或其他規管要求規定，部分特定事項不
     Directors and Senior Management of the Company. However,
                                                                                           允許全權委託他人負責，並須由全體董事會成員
     full delegation is not allowed for some specific matters under the
                                                                                           共同作出最終決定。董事會已採納如下明確之事
     Companies Ordinance, the Listing Rules, the CG Code or other                          項表，列載須待董事會作出決定之事項：
     regulatory requirements, and the final decisions on those specific
     matters are required to be taken by the whole board. A defined
     schedule of matters reserved for Board decisions has been
     adopted by the Board as follows:
                                                                                                      Code and policies of corporate governance practices

     The Board strives to achieve high standards of corporate                              董事會致力達致高標準的企業管治常規及履行本
     governance practices as well as the Company’s mission to creating                     公司為股東創造價值之使命。董事會負責制訂本
     value for our shareholders. The Board is responsible for developing                   公司之策略性方向，並持續監察本公司整體管理
     the strategic directions for the Company and continuous                               表現。制訂策略規劃已成為董事會實現使命和願
     monitoring of the performance of the general management of                            景以及維持長遠業務增長的重要職能，董事會因
     the Company. Strategic planning is one of the Board’s important                       而非常重視並致力制訂及執行策略規劃。
     functions for aligning mission and vision and sustaining long term
     business growth and lot of focus and attention have been devoted
     to formulation and execution of the strategic planning.

     Strategic planning horizons are currently set for five years. The                     策略規劃目前設定為五年。董事會已於二零一八
     Board has approved a Five Year Strategic Plan for the fiscal                          年九月批准截至二零二三╱二零二四財政年度止
     years up to 2023/2024 in September 2018 and has always been                           之五年策略規劃，並一直因應宏觀經濟、競爭性
     taking a proactive role in reviewing and revising the Strategic                       業務環境及監管規條之變動，積極定期檢討及修
     Plan periodically in response to changes in the macro economic,                       訂策略規劃。
     competitive business environment and regulatory requirements.

Board Diversity                                                                  董事會成員多元化
The Board has adopted a Board Diversity Policy to set out the                    董事會於二零一三年六月採納董事會成員多元化
objectives and the factors to be considered for achieving the                    政策，當中載列董事會成員多元化的目的以及達
diversity of the Board in June 2013. The Board Diversity Policy                  致多元化的考慮因素。董事會成員多元化政策已
has been published on the Company’s website.                                     刊登於本公司網站。

The Board recognises and embraces the benefits of having a                       董事會認同董事會成員多元化之裨益，並認為可
diverse Board to enhance the quality of its performance. Board                   增強董事會之表現質素。董事之委任及續聘過往
appointment and renewal have been, and will continue to be,                      根據並將繼續按候選人之功績以及能否補替及加
made based on merit as well as complementing and expanding                       強董事會整體之技能、知識及經驗為準則。董事
the skills, knowledge and experience of the Board as a whole. The                會已於年內修訂董事提名政策，除考慮個人經
Board has revised its Director Nomination Policy during the year                 驗、專業資格、文化背景、年齡等因素同時會考
to take into account the additional factors of independence and                  慮其獨立性及為董事會帶來的性別多元化，以及
gender diversity other than experience, professional qualification,              任何其他董事會於考慮董事之委任及續聘時認為
cultural, age, and any other factors that the Board might consider               有關之因素。薪酬及提名委員會已制定截至二零
relevant when considering Board appointment and renewal. The                     一九╱二零二零年度達致年齡及性別多元化的可
Remuneration and Nomination Committee has established the                        計量目標。於截至二零一九年三月三十一日止年
measurable objectives for achieving age and gender diversity up                  度內，達致該等目標之進度如下：
to the year of 2019/2020. As at the year ended 31st March 2019,
progress in achieving the objectives are:

     Corporate Governance Report
     企業管治報告




     The Board has approved a Whistleblowing Policy in March            於二零一二年三月，董事會已批准一項舉報政
     2012 to provide employees and external parties an open and         策，為僱員及外間人士提供一個公開及統一之舉
     unified reporting channel and procedures to report any serious     報渠道及程序，舉報任何涉及本公司及其僱員之
     misconduct or malpractice involving the Company and its            嚴重失當或不良行為。審核委員會獲委派全權負
     employees. The Audit Committee has been delegated with             責監察及檢討舉報政策之成效。年內，概無僱員
     the overall responsibility for monitoring and reviewing the        或外間人士舉報嚴重欺詐或失職事件而對本公司
     effectiveness of the Whistleblowing Policy. During the year,       之財務狀況及整體營運造成重大影響。
     no incident of serious fraud or misconduct was reported from
     employees nor external parties that had a material effect on the
     Company’s financial position and overall operations.

     The Board has adopted a Corporate Governance Policy to provide     董事會已採納一份企業管治政策，為本公司在制
     a basic framework for developing the Company’s policies and        定政策及常規方面建立了基本框架，確保能符合
     practices to ensure alignment of interests and expectations from   各股東、公眾投資者及其他持份者的利益與期
     our shareholders, the investing public and other stakeholders.     望。企業管治政策已刊登於本公司網站。
     The Corporate Governance Policy has been published on the
     Company’s website.

     The Audit Committee reviews regularly the corporate governance     審核委員會定期檢討本公司之企業管治架構及常
     structure and practices within the Company and monitors            規，並持續監察有關之合規情況。
     compliance fulfillment on an ongoing basis.

     Terms of reference of the Audit Committee are published on the     審核委員會之職權範圍已刊登於聯交所及本公司
     websites of the Stock Exchange and the Company. The Audit          之網站。審核委員會獲提供充足資源，包括獨立
     Committee is provided with sufficient resources, including         專業公司之意見（如需要），以履行其職責。
     the advice of independent professional firms, if necessary, to
     discharge its duties.

     The Audit Committee met three times in FY2018/2019. Each           審核委員會於二零一八╱二零一九財政年度內召
     member’s attendance record during the year is shown on page 46     開三次會議。各成員於年內出席會議之記錄列載
     of this Annual Report.                                             於本年報第 46 頁。

Evaluation of the Board and the Executive                             董事會及執行主席之評核
Chairman
The Board believes that evaluation of the Board and the Executive     董事會相信，對董事會及執行主席進行評核，乃
Chairman is an essential requirement of good corporate                良好企業管治之重要要求，並早於二零一一╱二
governance and has already adopted the Recommended Best               零一二財政年度開始採納企業管治守則中之建議
Practice for regular Board Evaluation under the CG Code as early      最佳常規定期為董事會作出評核。
in FY2011/2012.

The objective of the Board evaluation is to assist the Board in       董事會評核旨在協助董事會理解其長處及弱點並
identifying and addressing its strengths and weaknesses and           對其不足之處提出針對方案，以及突出董事會對
highlight the Board’s expectations for itself and areas for further   自身之期望及有待進一步改善之範疇。本公司亦
improvement. The Executive Chairman has also been evaluated           就執行主席作為董事會主席是否充分及有效地執
on whether he has adequately and effectively performed his roles      行其角色及履行其職責進行評核。董事會評核一
and fulfilled his responsibilities as the Chairman of the Board.      般每年進行一次。
Board evaluation is usually conducted on an annual basis.

A formal evaluation of the Board and the Executive Chairman           本公司已為董事會及執行主席進行二零一八╱二
was conducted for FY2018/2019. During the evaluation process,         零一九財政年度的正式評核。於評核過程中，本
questionnaire with scoring was used for collecting scores as well     公司採用計分問卷方式向全體董事收集評分及意
as opinions from all the Directors. The questionnaire included 20     見，當中包括 20 項評核董事會之題目及 10 項評
statements for Board evaluation and 10 statements for Executive       核執行主席之題目。填妥之評核表格以保密形式
Chairman evaluation. Completed evaluation forms were sent to          遞交予公司秘書。公司秘書其後就綜合評核結果
the Company Secretary on a confidential basis. The Company            編製摘要報告，並呈交於董事會會議上審閱及討
Secretary then prepared a summary report of the aggregate             論。
evaluation results for review and discussion in the Board meeting.

     The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk               董事會明白其肩負風險管理及內部監控系統之責
     management and internal control systems and reviewing their          任，並檢討其成效。該系統之設計旨在管理而非
     effectiveness. The systems are designed to manage rather than        消除無法達成業務目標之風險，並僅可為避免發
     eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business objectives, and    生重大錯誤陳述或損失提供合理而非絕對之保
     can only provide reasonable and not absolute assurance against       證。為實現此目標，董事會確保已制定有效之風
     material misstatement or loss. To achieve this, the Board ensures    險管治架構及持續風險管理程序，以達致本公司
     that there is an effective framework of risk governance and          業務之長久成功。
     ongoing risk management process to promote the long-term
     success of the Company.

     The Board is entrusted with the overall responsibility on an         董事會一直獲委以確保維持適當及有效之風險管
     ongoing basis for ensuring that appropriate and effective risk       理及內部監控之整體職責，從而提供合理保證，
     management and internal controls are maintained to provide           以避免發生資料之重大錯誤陳述。風險管理及內
     reasonable assurance against material misstatement of                部監控系統之主要特點如下 ︰
     information. Main features of the risk management and internal
     control systems are described in the sections below:

Risk Management Process                                                風險管理程序
Our risk management process is embedded in our daily operations,       我們之風險管理程序已納入日常營運範圍、財務
financial activities, compliance, strategy development & business      活動、合規、策略發展與業務規劃、投資決定及
planning, investment decision and external factors. The processes      外界因素，程序如下 ︰
are as follows:

1.   Anticipate: Potential risks and threats that are related to       1.   識別 ︰所有員工均參與識別不同業務流程
     different business processes and relevant to the Company’s             及與本公司目標相關之潛在風險及威脅。
     objectives will be anticipated and identified by all individual        為便於進行適當評估，我們將風險分為六
     staff members. Risks are grouped into six clusters (including          個範疇（包括信譽、策略、市場、流動資
     reputation, strategy, market, liquidity, credit and operation)         金、信貸及營運）。
     to facilitate the assessment appropriately.

2.   Rate: Each identified risk is evaluated on the basis of           2.   評估 ︰按照董事會設定之風險承受能力，
     impact and likelihood consistent with the risk appetite set            評估每項經識別風險的影響及可能性。風
     by the Board. The risk impact and likelihood will be rated             險之影響及可能性將根據過往記錄、統計
     according to the historical records, statistical analyses and          分析、相關經驗及管理層之專業判斷進行
     relevant experiences, specialist and expert judgments by               評級。集團風險管理團隊促進評估流程，
     management. Group Risk Management team facilitates the                 包括協助企業風險管理執行委員會及審核
     evaluation process including the summary discussion in the             委員會之討論。
     Enterprise Risk Management Executive Committee and
     Audit Committee.

3.   Respond: A delegated management is assigned to each risk          3.   控制 ︰各項風險將配對專責管理人員，成
     as risk owner to fully respond on each risk. Risk management           為風險負責人，以有效管理風險。風險負
     strategies and corresponding control owners are identified             責人制定風險管理策略以及指派監控負責
     by risk owner to deploy appropriate control activities.                人，實施適當監控活動。


4.   Regulate: We take a closed monitoring on the current status       4.   監察： 我們密切監控風險現況。各風險現
     of risk, which is being monitored through key risk indicators          況的結果取決於關鍵風險指標的預設量度
     with defined thresholds, occurrence of related incidents,              級別基準、所發生之相關事故、內部監控
     control effectiveness and audit results. Any change in                 程序之成效以及審計結果。風險現況倘出
     current status of risk to a worsened level will trigger a react        現任何不良轉變，均會觸發反應程序，展
     process through creation of a mitigation plan.                         開緩和計劃。


5.   React: When there is a change on the current status of risk,      5.   緩解： 當風險現況有所改變，相應負責人
     the corresponding owner will coordinate mitigation plan                將協調緩和計劃之行動細節、負責人及時
     with action details, responsible person and timeline to                間表，以處理風險情況。
     address the status.




                                                                                  Annual Report 2018/2019 年度報告   61
     Corporate Governance Report
     企業管治報告




     6.   Report: To support the oversight of the Board on the            6.   通報︰風險報告內容及風險管理框架，均獲
          effectiveness of Vitasoy’s risk management, risk reporting           董事會批准，以支持董事會監察維他奶風
          requirement was approved by the Board of Directors under             險管理之成效。集團風險管理定期向企業
          risk management framework. Periodic risk report is provided          風險管理執行委員會提供風險報告，監控

     Dividend Policy                                                          股息政策
     The Board has adopted a Dividend Policy to provide guidance to           董事會已採納股息政策，就派發盈餘資金予股東
     the Board and the management of the Company for distribution             向董事會及管理層提供指引。
     of surplus funds to its shareholders.

     In determining the level of dividend payment, the following              就決定所派付的股息水平，須計及下列準則及考
     criteria and consideration must be taken:                                慮因素：


     –    Sharing of success with shareholders                                –   與股東共享成果
     –    Liquidity and capital requirement                                   –   流動資金及資本要求
     –    Historical trend of dividend payout                                 –   過往股息派付之趨勢
     –    Benchmarking with competitors within the industry                   –   與業內競爭對手之標準比較
     –    Fulfilment of financial covenants                                   –   符合財務契約
     –    Taxation impact                                                     –   稅務影響
     –    Restrictions                                                        –   限制條件
     –    Other factors that may deem relevant                                –   任何其他視為相關之因素


     The Company intends to declare dividends to shareholders semi-           本公司計劃每半年向股東宣派股息，每年宣派之
     annually in an aggregate amount per year of approximately 60%            股息總額預計為扣除一次性事件影響後之股東預
     of the anticipated consolidated net annual profit attributable           期應佔年度綜合溢利約 60%，並可能除中期或末
     to shareholders net of one-off event impact and may declare              期股息外不時宣派特別股息。董事會將不時酌情
     special dividends from time to time in addition to interim or            決定是否派發股息以及所派發股息之水平。
     final dividends. The determination to pay dividends and level of
     dividend to be distributed will be subject to the discretion of the
     Board from time to time.

     On 4th September 2002, the Company adopted a share option             於二零零二年九月四日，本公司採納一項購股權
     scheme (the “2002 Share Option Scheme”) under which the               計劃（「二零零二年購股權計劃」），據此，董事
     Directors might, at their discretion, grant options to employees      可酌情向本公司或其任何附屬公司之僱員及董事
     and Directors of the Company or any of its subsidiaries to            授予購股權以認購本公司之普通股。二零零二年
     subscribe for ordinary shares in the Company. The 2002 Share          購股權計劃之有效期為十年，於二零一二年九月
     Option Scheme was valid for 10 years ended 3rd September 2012.        三日終止。該計劃旨在獎勵及回饋對本集團作出
     The purpose of this scheme was to provide incentives and rewards      貢獻之僱員。該計劃之主要條款如下：
     to employees for their contribution to the Group. The principal
     terms of this scheme were as follows:

     –    The exercise price of the options is the higher of (a) the       –     購股權之行使價為以下之較高者：(a) 股份

          Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”) on the date of grant,              合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）錄得之收
          which must be a business day; and (b) the average of the               市價；及 (b) 股份於緊接授出日期前五個營
          closing prices of the shares on the Stock Exchange for the             業日在聯交所錄得之平均收市價。
          five business days immediately preceding the date of grant.

     –    The options were exercisable for a period to be notified         –     購股權之行使期限由董事知會每名購股權
          by the Directors to each option holder, such period not to             持有人，該期限由授出日期起計不得超過
          exceed 10 years from the date of grant.                                十年。


     –    There was no minimum period which an option must be held         –     購股權在歸屬期內毋須先行持有一段最短
          before it could be exercised during the vesting period, but            期限方可行使，惟董事獲授權可酌情於要
          the Directors were empowered to impose at their discretion             約日期時附加任何最短持有期。
          any such minimum period at the date of offer.

     –    The maximum number of shares which may be issued upon            –     就行使全部將授予之購股權而可予發行之
          exercise of all options to be granted must not exceed 10% of           股份數目，最多不得超過批准及採納計劃
          the shares in issue on the date of approval and adoption of            當日之已發行股份 10%。

     On 28th August 2012, the Company adopted a new share option            本公司於二零一二年八月二十八日採納新購股權
     scheme (the “2012 Share Option Scheme”). The purpose of the            計劃（「二零一二年購股權計劃」）。二零一二年
     2012 Share Option Scheme is to recognise and acknowledge the           購股權計劃旨在肯定及表揚本公司或其任何附
     contributions which the Directors, executives and employees of         屬公司之董事、行政人員及僱員（「合資格參與
     the Company or any of its subsidiaries (“Eligible Participants”)       者」）對本集團已作出或將作出之貢獻。該計劃
     have made or will make to the Group. It will provide the Eligible      將讓合資格參與者持有本公司股份，以激勵合資
     Participants with an opportunity to have a personal stake in the       格參與者為本集團之利益提升其表現及效率；以
     Company with a view to motivating the Eligible Participants to         及吸引及挽留所作出之貢獻對或將對本集團長遠
     excel their performance and efficiency for the benefit of the Group;   增長有所裨益之合資格參與者，或與彼等維持持
     and attracting and retaining or otherwise maintaining an ongoing       續之關係。董事會可酌情向本公司或其附屬公司
     relationship with the Eligible Participants whose contributions        之任何董事、行政人員或僱員授予購股權。
     are or will be beneficial to the long term growth of the Group.
     The Board may at its discretion grant options to any Director,
     executive or employee of the Company or its subsidiaries.

     The principal terms of the 2012 Share Option Scheme were as            二零一二年購股權計劃之主要條款如下：
     follows:

     –    The exercise price of the options is the higher of (a) the        –     購股權之行使價為以下之較高者：(a) 股份
          closing price of the shares on the Stock Exchange on the date           於授出日期（當日必須為營業日）在聯交所
          of grant, which must be a business day; and (b) the average             錄得之收市價；及 (b) 股份於緊接授出日期
          of the closing prices of the shares on the Stock Exchange for           前五個營業日在聯交所錄得之平均收市價。
          the five business days immediately preceding the date of
          grant.

     –    The period within which the options must be exercised             –     本公司將於授予購股權時指定購股權須予
          would be specified by the Company at the time of grant. This            行使之期限。此屆滿期限須不遲於有關授
          period must expire no later than 10 years from the relevant             予日期起計十年。
          date of grant.

     –    There was no minimum period for which an option must              –     在歸屬期內，並無購股權必須持有之最短
          be held nor a performance target which must be achieved                 期限或購股權可行使前必須達到之表現目
          before it could be exercised during the vesting period, but             標，惟董事會可就授予購股權自行酌情釐
          the Board may determine at its sole discretion any such                 定任何有關條款。
          terms on the grant of an option.

     –    The maximum number of shares which may be issued upon             –     就行使全部將授予之購股權而可予發行之
          exercise of all options to be granted must not exceed 10% of            股份數目，最多不得超過批准及採納計劃
          the shares in issue on the date of approval and adoption of             當日之已發行股份 10%。

Auditor’s responsibilities for the audit of                            核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔
the consolidated financial statements                                  的責任
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether        我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在
the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from         由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得合理
material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to           保證，並出具包括我們意見的核數師報告。我們
issue an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. This report is    是按照香港《公司條例》第 405 條的規定，僅向
made solely to you, as a body, in accordance with section 405 of       整體成員報告。除此以外，我們的報告不可用作
the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance, and for no other purpose.           其他用途。我們概不就本報告的內容，對任何其
We do not assume responsibility towards or accept liability to any     他人士負責或承擔法律責任。
other person for the contents of this report.

Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a        合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按照《香
guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs             港審計準則》進行的審計，在某一重大錯誤陳述
will always detect a material misstatement when it exists.             存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引
Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are considered         起，如果合理預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響
material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably   綜合財務報表使用者依賴財務報表所作出的經濟
be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken on      決定，則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。
the basis of these consolidated financial statements.

throughout the audit. We also:                                         亦：

     the consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud            務報表存在重大錯誤陳述的風險，設計及
     or error, design and perform audit procedures responsive               執行審計程序以應對這些風險，以及獲取
     to those risks, and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient           充足和適當的審計憑證，作為我們意見的
     and appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion. The                基礎。由於欺詐可能涉及串謀、偽造、蓄
     risk of not detecting a material misstatement resulting                意遺漏、虛假陳述，或凌駕於內部控制之
     from fraud is higher than for one resulting from error, as             上，因此未能發現因欺詐而導致的重大錯
     fraud may involve collusion, forgery, intentional omissions,           誤陳述的風險高於未能發現因錯誤而導致
     misrepresentations, or the override of internal control.               的重大錯誤陳述的風險。

are not readily apparent from other sources. Actual results may

ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised          計估計之修訂只影響某一期間，則該修訂便會在
in the period in which the estimate is revised if the revision affects   估計修訂期間內確認，或如果修訂對當期和未來
only that period, or in the period of the revision and future periods    期間均有影響，則在作出修訂之期間和未來期間

      for the amendments to HKFRS 9, Prepayment features with negative         「具有負補償特性之預付款項」之修訂除外，而該

      HKFRS 9.

      measurement of credit losses and hedge accounting. The Group             貸虧損計量及對沖會計處理之影響。本集團亦已
      has also been impacted by HKFRS 15 in relation to accounting for         受到《香港財務報告準則》第 15 號有關應付客戶
      consideration payable to customer and presentation of contract           代價之會計處理以及合約資產及合約負債呈列之

      policies are discussed in note 1(c)(ii) for HKFRS 9 and note 1(c)(iii)   財務報告準則》第 15 號之會計政策變動詳情分別
      for HKFRS 15.                                                            於附註 1(c)(ii) 及附註 1(c)(iii) 內討論。


      (ii)   HKFRS 9, Financial instruments, including the                     (ii) 《香港財務報告準則》第 9 號「金融工
             amendments to HKFRS 9, Prepayment features with                         具」，包括《香港財務報告準則》第 9 號
             negative compensation                                                  「具有負補償特性之預付款項」之修訂
      HKFRS 9 replaces HKAS 39, Financial instruments: recognition and         《香港財務報告準則》第 9 號取代《香港會計準則》
      measurement. It sets out the requirements for recognising and            第 39 號「金融工具：確認及計量」。其載列確認
      measuring financial assets, financial liabilities and some contracts     及計量金融資產、金融負債及若干買賣非金融項
      to buy or sell non-financial items.                                      目合約之規定。


      (a) Credit losses                                                        (a)   信貸虧損
      HKFRS 9 replaces the “incurred loss” model in HKAS 39 with the           《香港財務報告準則》第 9 號以預期信貸虧損模式
      expected credit losses (“ECL”) model. The ECL model requires an          取代《香港會計準則》第 39 號之「已產生虧損」模
      ongoing measurement of credit risk associated with a financial           式。預期信貸虧損模式規定持續計量金融資產相
      asset and therefore recognises ECLs earlier than under the               關之信貸風險，因此，確認預期信貸虧損之時間
      “incurred loss” accounting model in HKAS 39.                             較根據《香港會計準則》第 39 號之「已產生虧損」
                                                                               會計模式確認之時間為早。


      The Group applies the new ECL model to financial assets measured         本集團將新預期信貸虧損模式應用於按攤銷成本
      at amortised cost (including cash and cash equivalents, trade and        計量之金融資產（包括現金及現金等值項目、應收
      other receivables and loan to joint venture).                            賬款及其他應收款以及向合營公司借出之貸款）
                                                                                                   。


      For further details on the Group’s accounting policy for accounting      有關本集團對信貸虧損會計處理之會計政策之進
      for credit losses, see note 1(k)(i).                                     一步詳情，見附註 1(k)(i)。


      (b) Hedge accounting                                                     (b)   對沖會計處理
      The Group has elected to adopt the new general hedge accounting          本集團已選擇採納《香港財務報告準則》第 9 號之
      model in HKFRS 9. Depending on the complexity of the hedge,              新一般對沖會計模式。視乎對沖之複雜程度，此
      this new accounting model allows a more qualitative approach             項新會計模式與應用《香港會計準則》第 39 號相
      to assessing hedge effectiveness compared to HKAS 39 to be               比，為評估對沖之有效性提供更為定性之方法，
      applied, and the assessment is always forward looking.                   且有關評估更具前瞻性。

             transferred, the amount of any non-controlling interest in              控股權益金額與本集團先前持有被收購方

             liabilities measured as at the acquisition date.                        負債之公允值淨額之部份。

      immediately in profit or loss as a gain on a bargain purchase.          為議價收購之收益。

The gross carrying amount of a financial asset is written off (either     倘金融資產並無實際收回前景，則其賬面總值
partially or in full) to the extent that there is no realistic prospect   （部分或全部）將會被撇銷。該情況通常出現在本
of recovery. This is generally the case when the Group determines         集團認為債務人並無資產或收入來源可產生足夠
that the debtor does not have assets or sources of income that            現金流量以償還將予撇銷之金額。
could generate sufficient cash flows to repay the amounts subject
to the write-off.

The adoption of the ECL model under HKFRS 9 has no material               採納《香港財務報告準則》第 9 號之預期信貸虧損
impact on the Group.                                                      模式並無對本集團造成重大影響。


(b) Policy applicable prior to 1st April 2018                             (b)   於二零一八年四月一日前適用之政策
Prior to 1st April 2018, an “incurred loss” model was used to             於二零一八年四月一日前，本集團採納「已產生
measure impairment losses on financial assets not classified              虧損」模式計量並非分類為按公允值計入損益之
as at fair value through profit or loss (e.g. trade and other             金融資產之減值虧損（例如：應收賬款及其他應
receivables, available-for-sale investments and held-to-maturity          收款、可供出售投資及持有至到期債務證券）。
debt securities). Under the “incurred loss” model, an impairment          根據「已產生虧損」模式，僅在出現客觀減值證
loss was recognised only when there was objective evidence of             據時方確認減值虧損。客觀減值證據包括：
impairment. Objective evidence of impairment included:

–     significant financial difficulties of the debtor;                   –     債務人有重大財務困難；


–     a breach of contract, such as a default or delinquency in           –     違反合約，如拖欠利息或本金還款；
      interest or principal payments;

–     it becoming probable that the debtor will enter bankruptcy          –     債務人可能破產或進行其他財務重組；
      or other financial reorganisation;

–     significant changes in the technological, market, economic or       –     科技、市場、經濟或法律環境有重大改變
      legal environment that have an adverse effect on the debtor;              以致對債務人造成不利影響；及
      and

–     a significant or prolonged decline in the fair value of an          –     股本工具投資之公允值大幅或長期下跌至
      investment in an equity instrument below its cost.                        低於其成本。

(d) Deferred tax assets                                                (d) 遞延稅項資產
Deferred tax assets are recognised for unused tax losses and           遞延稅項資產乃就未動用稅務虧損及可抵扣暫時
temporary deductible differences. As those deferred tax assets         差額而確認。由於該等遞延稅項資產只限於有可
can only be recognised to the extent that it is probable that future   能使用未動用稅收抵免來抵銷日後應課稅溢利時
taxable profit will be available against which the unused tax          才會確認，因此需要管理層判斷日後獲得應課稅
benefits can be utilised, management’s judgements is required          溢利之可能性。本集團不斷審閱管理層之評估，
to assess the probability of future taxable profits. Management’s      倘未來應課稅溢利能使遞延稅項資產被收回，便
assessment is constantly reviewed and additional deferred tax          會確認額外之遞延稅項資產。遞延稅項資產之性
assets are recognised if it becomes probable that future taxable       質及賬面值詳情於附註 24(b) 披露。
profits will allow the deferred tax assets to be recovered. Details
of the nature and carrying amounts of deferred tax assets are
disclosed in note 24(b).

returns, rebates and discounts.                                        扣。

is presented as entire revenue of the Group is derived from            品，並於某一時間點確認，故並無呈列客戶合約
manufacture and sale of food and beverages, and is recognised at       收入細分。

      (e) The fair value of investment properties                            (e) 投資物業之公允值
      The fair value of investment properties at 31st March 2019 is          投資物業於二零一九年三月三十一日之公允值為
      $65,900,000 (2018: $63,200,000) which is estimated at their            65,900,000 元（ 二 零 一 八 年：63,200,000 元），
      open market value by reference to recent market transactions in        此乃經參考可比較物業之近期市場交易後按公開
      comparable properties (2018: same basis of valuation adopted).         市場價值進行估計（二零一八年：採取同一估值
      The valuation was carried out by an independent firm of surveyors,     基準）。估值乃由獨立測量師行羅馬國際評估有
      Roma Appraisals Limited, who have among their staff members of         限公司進行，其部份職員為香港測量師學會之會
      the Hong Kong Institute of Surveyors with recent experience in         員，在所估值物業之地區及類別具有相關近期經
      the location and category of the properties being valued.              驗。

      The fair value measurement is categorised as a Level 3 valuation        公允值計量根據《香港財務報告準則》第 13 號
      under the three-level fair value hierarchy as defined in HKFRS         「公允值計量 」所界定之三級公允值架構分類為第
      13, Fair value measurement. The level into which a fair value           三級估值。將某公允值計量分類之等級乃經參考
      measurement is classified is determined with reference to               如下估值方法所用數據之可觀察性及重要性後釐
      observability and significance of the inputs used in the valuation      定：
      technique as follows:

      –       Level 1 valuations: Fair value measured using only Level 1     –   第一級估值：僅使用第一級輸入數據（即於
              inputs i.e. unadjusted quoted prices in active markets for         計量日同類資產或負債於活躍市場之未經
              identical assets or liabilities at the measurement date;           調整報價）計量之公允值；

      –       Level 2 valuations: Fair value measured using Level 2 inputs   –   第二級估值：使用第二級輸入數據（即未能
              i.e. observable inputs which fail to meet Level 1, and not         達到第一級之可觀察輸入數據）且並未使用
              using significant unobservable inputs. Unobservable inputs         重大不可觀察輸入數據計量之公允值。不
              are inputs for which market data are not available; and            可觀察輸入數據為無市場數據之輸入數據；
                                                                                 及

      –       Level 3 valuations: Fair value measured using significant      –   第三級估值：使用重大不可觀察輸入數據
              unobservable inputs.                                               計量之公允值。

      The fair value of investment properties is determined by using         投資物業之公允值乃參考可比較物業之市價使用
      direct comparison approach with reference to the market price          直接比較法釐定，並就參考交易之樓宇質素及時
      of comparable properties and adjusted for building quality and         間作出調整。公允值計量之重大不可觀察輸入
      timing of the reference transaction. The significant unobservable      數據乃個別物業調整率，介乎 0.95 至 1.05（二零
      input in the fair value measurement is the property-specific           一八年：0.94 至 1.05）。公允值計量與個別物業
      adjusting rate, which ranged from 0.95 to 1.05 (2018: 0.94 to          調整率之間成正向關係。
      1.05). The fair value measurement is positively correlated to the
      property-specific adjusting rate.

(f) Capital management                                                 (f)   資本管理
The Group’s primary objectives when managing capital are to            本集團在管理資本時之首要目標為保障本集團能
safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern,          持續經營的能力，透過將產品及服務的價格訂於
so that it can continue to provide returns for shareholders and        與風險相稱的水平及按合理成本籌措融資，使其
benefits for other stakeholders, by pricing products and services      能繼續為股東提供回報及為其他利益相關人士提
commensurately with the level of risk and by securing access to        供利益。
finance at a reasonable cost.

The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages its capital       本集團積極及定期對資本架構進行檢討及管理，
structure to maintain a balance between the higher shareholder         以在較高股東回報可能附帶較高借貸水平與雄厚
returns that might be possible with higher levels of borrowings        資本狀況帶來之優勢及保障間取得平衡，並因應
and the advantages and security afforded by a sound capital            經濟環境之變化對資本架構作出調整。
position, and makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of
changes in economic conditions.

Consistent with industry practices, the Group monitors its capital     本著業內慣例，本集團以負債對經調整資本比率
structure on the basis of a debt-to-adjusted capital ratio. For        作為監控其資本架構之基準。就此目的，本集團
this purpose, the Group defines debt as the total of bank loans        將負債界定為銀行貸款及融資租賃債務之總額，
and obligations under finance leases, and adjusted capital as all      經調整資本為權益之所有組成部份減非累計擬派
components of equity less unaccrued proposed dividends.                股息。

26 Financial risk management and fair                                    26 財務風險管理及金融工具之公
   values of financial instruments                                          允值
Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency risks          本集團在一般業務過程中存在信貸、流動資金、
arises in the normal course of the Group’s business. The Group’s         利率及貨幣風險。本集團面臨之有關風險及本集
exposures to these risks and the financial risk management               團就管理該等風險所採用之財務風險管理政策與
policies and practices used by the Group to manage these risks           慣例載述如下。
are described below.

(a) Credit risk                                                          (a) 信貸風險
Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will default on its   信貸風險指交易方違反其合約責任而導致本集團
contractual obligations resulting in a financial loss to the Group.      財務虧損之風險。


The Group’s credit risk is primarily attributable to trade and other     本集團之信貸風險主要來自應收賬款及其他應收
receivables. Management has a credit policy in place and the             款。管理層備有信貸政策，而所面臨之有關信貸
exposure to this credit risk is monitored on an ongoing basis.           風險持續受到監控。


The Group’s cash and bank deposits are placed with financial             本集團之現金及銀行存款乃存放於有良好信貸評
institutions with sound credit ratings, and the management               級之金融機構，管理層認為本集團面臨之信貸風
consider the Group’s exposure to credit risk is low.                     險為低。


The Group does not provide any guarantees which would expose             本集團並無提供任何將令本集團面臨信貸風險之
the Group to credit risk.                                                擔保。


Trade debtors and bills receivable (which are included in                應收賬款及應收票據（已包括於應收賬款及
trade and other receivables)                                             其他應收款內）
The Group’s exposure to credit risk is influenced mainly by the          本集團面臨之信貸風險主要受各客戶之個別情況
individual characteristics of each customer rather than the              而非客戶經營業務所在之行業或國家影響，因
industry or country in which the customers operate and therefore         此，重大集中信貸風險主要於本集團面臨個別客
significant concentrations of credit risk primarily arise when the       戶之重大風險時出現。於匯報日，本集團最大客
Group has significant exposure to individual customers. At the end       戶及五大客戶之欠款分別佔應收賬款及其他應收
of reporting period, 16.9% (2018: 17.0%) and 37.1% (2018: 38.9%)         款總額之 16.9%（二零一八年：17.0%）及 37.1%
of the total trade and other receivables was due from the Group’s        （二零一八年：38.9%）。
largest customer and the five largest customers respectively.

Individual credit evaluations are performed on all customers             所有要求超過特定信貸金額之客戶均須進行個別
requiring credit over a certain amount. These evaluations focus          信貸評估。該等評估集中於客戶過往之到期還款
on the customer’s past history of making payments when due and           記錄及現時還款能力，並會考慮客戶之特定資料
current ability to pay, and take into account information specific to    以及有關客戶營運所在之經濟環境。應收賬款及
the customer as well as pertaining to the economic environment in        應收票據一般於發單日起計一至三個月內到期。
which the customer operates. Trade debtors and bills receivables         一般而言，本集團不會從客戶取得抵押品。
are generally due within one to three months from the date
of billing. Normally, the Group does not obtain collateral from
customers.

      (a) Transactions with related parties (Continued)                     (a) 與關連人士之交易（續）
      The related party transactions in respect of (i) and (ii) above       上述有關第 (i) 及 (ii) 項之關連人士交易構成上市
      constitute continuing connected transactions as defined in            規則第 14A 章所界定之持續關連交易。然而，由
